=== Day 0: 2026-07-27 (mon) [Refeed: false] ===
[8:00-8:10 am] Wakeup
  - Use bathroom
  - Weight (Step on scale)
  - Drink cold water
  - Take 250mg Citicoline (w/ 25mg Tyrosine)
[8:10-8:30 am] Get Ready
  - Brush teeth
  - Blotting Sheet (Place over oily areas and press against skin for 3s, lift and press - no rubbing ever) to absorb heavy overnight sebum
  - Gently splash face/neck with water for 5-10s to rinse off overnight sweat -> Pat dry with new bamboo baby washcloth
  - Ascorbyl 12% (Apply to slightly damp skin, AVOID orbital bone): 5 drops (1 forehead, 1 left cheek, 1 right cheek, 2 neck) -> WAIT 90s
  - Eye Serum FIRST: Gently tap 1 drop of The Ordinary Caffeine Solution across orbital bone -> Skin1004 Centella Sun Serum: Apply face in 2 layers (0.5 finger, wait 30s, 0.5 finger) -> Apply 1 finger for neck -> WAIT 3m
  - Get dressed
[8:30-8:50 am] Morning Dine
  - Eat in front of Verilux HappyLight (45-degree angle to face):
  - 1/3 serving of my pasta
  - 2 Boiled Eggs
  - 2 standalone K2 (100mcg each)
  - 1 cap Biotin+Keratin (10mg/500mg)
  - 1 softgel Vitamin E (267mg)
  - 1 cap Zinc Picolinate (30mg)
  - Swish/drink water after eating/drinking to clear mouth
[8:50-1:00 pm] Work
  - Laundry Cycle 1: Bamboo Washcloths & Cotton Pillowcases (3/5 warmness) -> 1-2 tbsp All Free Clear (Liquid). NO Fabric Softener (Dry on LOW heat)
  - Run Dishwasher
[1:00-1:30 pm] Lunch
  - 2/3 serving of my pasta
  - Take 3 caps Calcium Citrate
[1:30-5:00 pm] Work
  - 4:00 pm Touch-up: Wash hands -> Blotting Sheet (Place over oily area and press against skin for 3s, lift and press - no rubbing ever) -> Wash hands -> House of Hur Sun Fluid: Apply face in 2 layers (0.5 finger, wait 30s, 0.5 finger) -> Apply 1 finger for neck -> WAIT 3m
  - Laundry Cycle 2: Bath Towels & Body Washcloths (5/5 warmness) -> Wash 1: 2 tbsp All Free Clear (Liquid) + 1/3 cup Bleach -> Wash 2: Full Cycle (WATER ONLY - no detergent) to lift bleach residue (Dry on LOW heat)
[5:00-5:55 pm] Study Time
  - Study (Beat traffic before gym)
[5:55-6:30 pm] Get Dressed & Commute to Gym (Resistance)
  - Get dressed for gym
  - 🍌 Eat 1 Medium Banana (Pre-Workout)
  - Mix 1 scoop Cluster Dextrin + 1 scoop whey isolate (30g protein) in water bottle (+ 0.5 tsp Lite Salt electrolytes). Drink 1/3 on commute, sip rest during workout
[6:30-8:00 pm] Legs (Hamstrings / Glutes)
[8:00-8:30 pm] Commute Home
[8:50-9:20 pm] Dinner
  - 1 scoop creatine monohydrate (5g)
  - Cook Salmon (3 portions): start 1st portion (eat 1/2, save 1/2 in fridge)
  - 🥕 Eat 1/7th of a bag (~14 baby carrots)
  - 2 cap Ashwagandha
[9:20-9:50 pm] Chores & Clean Up
  - 1 scoop Casein in 16oz water -> Swish/drink water after
  - The 7 bottles water are separate from this. This is in addition to that. So 7 bottles + this.
  - Eat 1 Kiwi now (30m before bed prep to protect enamel) & Swish water
[9:50-10:20 pm] Shower & Bed Prep
  - 🛑 Grab a FRESH Bath Towel for the new rotation; use instead of bamboo!
  - Set Dehumidifier to 55%
  - Brush teeth (safe since 30m since kiwi)
  - Pre-Shower: Rub 2-3 pumps dedicated Cleansing Oil on DRY face (30s) to melt sunscreen/sweat
  - In-Shower: Wet hands, rub face/neck/ears to emulsify oil (15-20s) -> Rinse thoroughly
  - Hair: 60s warm massage
  - Body: Hold Washcloth under water until SOAKING wet. Apply Dove Deep Moisture Body Wash into cloth and rub together to build a rich foam cushion. Gently sweep foamy cloth over upper and lower body (for frictionless gym maintenance)
  - Face: Lather 1 pump of Lipikar AP+ (Barrier Shield) in wet hands for 3-4s to build foam. Gently glide foam onto face/neck/ears with LUKEWARM water for EXACTLY 30s (then rinse)
  - Finish: Lukewarm rinse
  - Pat face & beard completely dry with the FRESH Bath Towel (Do Skincare immediately while bathroom is humid, before drying your body)
[10:20-10:50 pm] Night Supplement + Read/Reflect
  - Paula's Choice 2% BHA (Apply to BONE DRY skin to prevent irritation, AVOID orbital bone): 3 drops (1 forehead, 1 left cheek, 1 right cheek) -> WAIT 1-2m
  - Underarms: Ensure pits are BONE DRY -> Moisturize with face moisturizer (Vanicream Daily Facial) -> WAIT to dry -> Apply 5% AHA Deodorant
  - Eye Serum FIRST: Gently tap 1 drop of The Ordinary Caffeine Solution across orbital bone
  - Moisturizer (Cetaphil Hydrating Lotion): Apply 1.5 pumps for face, 1.5 pumps for neck, and 0.5 pumps for ears, then rub in -> WAIT to dry
  - Take 3 Magnesium Glycinate + 1 tablet Finasteride (1mg)
[10:50-11:20 pm] Fall Asleep
  - Come up with todo list for tom
  - Lights out, try to fall asleep within 30 min

[9:50-10:20 pm] Shower & Bed Prep
  - Set Dehumidifier to 55%
  - Brush teeth (safe since 30m since kiwi)
  - Pre-Shower: Rub 2-3 pumps dedicated Cleansing Oil on DRY face (30s) to melt sunscreen/sweat
  - In-Shower: Wet hands, rub face/neck/ears to emulsify oil (15-20s) -> Rinse thoroughly
  - Hair: 60s warm massage
  - Body: Hold Washcloth under water until SOAKING wet. Apply Dove Deep Moisture Body Wash into cloth and rub together to build a rich foam cushion. Gently sweep foamy cloth over upper and lower body (for frictionless gym maintenance)
  - Face: Lather 1 pump of Vanicream Gentle Cleanser in wet hands for 3-4s to build foam. Gently glide foam onto face/neck/ears with LUKEWARM water for EXACTLY 30s (then rinse)
  - Finish: Lukewarm rinse
  - Pat face & beard completely dry with a new bamboo baby washcloth (Do Skincare immediately while bathroom is humid)
[10:20-10:50 pm] Night Supplement + Read/Reflect
  - Joseon Alpha-Arbutin (Apply to slightly damp skin, AVOID orbital bone): 4 drops (1 forehead, 1 left cheek, 1 right cheek, 1 neck) -> WAIT 1-2m

  - Eye Serum FIRST: Gently tap 1 drop of The Ordinary Caffeine Solution across orbital bone

  - Take 3 Magnesium Glycinate + 1 tablet Finasteride (1mg)

  - Lights out, try to fall asleep within 30 min

[9:50-10:20 pm] Shower & Bed Prep
  - Set Dehumidifier to 55%
  - Brush teeth (safe since 30m since kiwi)
  - Pre-Shower: Rub 2-3 pumps dedicated Cleansing Oil on DRY face (30s) to melt sunscreen/sweat
  - In-Shower: Wet hands, rub face/neck/ears to emulsify oil (15-20s) -> Rinse thoroughly
  - Hair: 60s warm massage
  - Body: Hold Washcloth under water until SOAKING wet. Apply Dove Deep Moisture Body Wash into cloth and rub together to build a rich foam cushion. Gently sweep foamy cloth over upper and lower body (for frictionless gym maintenance)
  - Face: Lather 1 pump of Vanicream Gentle Cleanser in wet hands for 3-4s to build foam. Gently glide foam onto face/neck/ears with LUKEWARM water for EXACTLY 30s (then rinse)
  - Finish: Lukewarm rinse
  - Pat face & beard completely dry with a new bamboo baby washcloth (Do Skincare immediately while bathroom is humid)
[10:20-10:50 pm] Night Supplement + Read/Reflect
  - Joseon Alpha-Arbutin (Apply to slightly damp skin, AVOID orbital bone): 4 drops (1 forehead, 1 left cheek, 1 right cheek, 1 neck) -> WAIT 1-2m
  - Underarms: Ensure pits are BONE DRY -> Moisturize with face moisturizer (Vanicream Daily Facial) -> WAIT to dry -> Apply 5% AHA Deodorant
  - Eye Serum FIRST: Gently tap 1 drop of The Ordinary Caffeine Solution across orbital bone
  - Moisturizer (Cetaphil Hydrating Lotion): Apply 1.5 pumps for face, 1.5 pumps for neck, and 0.5 pumps for ears, then rub in -> WAIT to dry
  - Take 3 Magnesium Glycinate + 1 tablet Finasteride (1mg)

  - Lights out, try to fall asleep within 30 min

  - Finish: Lukewarm rinse
  - Pat face & beard completely dry with a new bamboo baby washcloth (Do Skincare immediately while bathroom is humid)

[9:50-10:20 pm] Shower & Bed Prep
  - 🛑 Grab a FRESH Bath Towel for the new rotation; use instead of bamboo!
  - Set Dehumidifier to 55%
  - Brush teeth (safe since 30m since kiwi)
  - Pre-Shower: Rub 2-3 pumps dedicated Cleansing Oil on DRY face (30s) to melt sunscreen/sweat

  - Hair: 60s warm massage
  - Body: Hold Washcloth under water until SOAKING wet. Apply Dove Deep Moisture Body Wash into cloth and rub together to build a rich foam cushion. Gently sweep foamy cloth over upper and lower body (for frictionless gym maintenance)

  - Take 3 Magnesium Glycinate + 1 tablet Finasteride (1mg)

  - Pat face & beard completely dry with a new bamboo baby washcloth (Do Skincare immediately while bathroom is humid)
